use both\");\n  }\n}\n\nfunction assertEnvSet(\n  loc: OptionPath,\n  value: unknown,\n): void | EnvSet<ValidatedOptions> {\n  if (loc.parent.type === \"env\") {\n    throw new Error(`${msg(loc)} is
